       IDENTIFICATION DIVISION. 
       PROGRAM-ID.       CCR12BKD.  
       AUTHOR.           KEVAN HARRISON.
       INSTALLATION.     CARNIVAL CRUISE LINES. 
       DATE-WRITTEN.     APRIL 96.  
       DATE-COMPILED.   
      *REMARKS.          THIS PROGRAM DISPLAYS BOOKINGS 
      ********************************************************************  
      **    Normal CCR12BKD Program logic (simplified):                 **  
      **    After determining when a valid AGENT has processed the      **  
      **    SABRE request, there are two paths for logic flow           **  
      **    (para 0500-):                                               **  
      **    I. NAME REQUEST (Paras 1000- thru 1300-).                   **  
      **       PAX-NAME-REC fetched using key fields then LNAME and     **  
      **       FIRST-NAME-INIT are compared to SABRE name entered for   **  
      **       EXACT match until either:                                **  
      **         A. SINGLE or MULTIPLE bookings are found               **  
      **            1. SINGLE - passes on to para 2000-                 **  
      **            2. MULTIPLE - passes entries back to SABRE          **  
      **               for a subsequent SABRE selection                 *   
      **            3. Both are (re)processed as BOOKING REQUESTs       **  
      **               using the PAX-BOOK-NUM                           **  
      **         B. MAX TIME (1500 SUPS) is reached (Para 1100-)        **  
      **            (same process as a MULTIPLE BOOKING).               **  
      **                                                                **  
      **     II. BOOKING REQUEST (PARA 2000- thru END).                 **  
      **         INDV-BOOKING-REC is fetched along with member          **  
      **         PAX-REC records to select the data to be passed back   **  
      **         to SABRE.                                              **  
      **         A.  The BEST-FARE-TABLE is created in para 3210-       **  
      **             and accessed to provide FARE and PRICE data.       **  
      **         B.  The BEST-FARE-TABLE can be recreated in Para 4300  **  
      **             if EXTRA PAX depart from different city with a     **  
      **             different fare-code than the first PAX...          **  
      ********************************************************************  
      **             M A I N T E N A N C E     L O G                    **  
      ********************************************************************

C19412 2175-CALC-PAX-OBC-AMT.   
CCLAUS*    MOVE 1 TO WS-SUB2.   
CCLAUS     MOVE 0 TO WS-DK-4
CCLAUS               WS-SUB2.   
CCLAUS*    MOVE 1 TO WS-DK-4.   
CCLAUS*    IF SELRET-EDOCS AND RSP06-PAX-TICKET-DATE (WS-DK-4) > ZEROES 
CCLAUS*        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EDOCS-PAX-TICKET-IND  
CCLAUS*    END-IF.  
CCLAUS*    IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-CODE (WS-DK-4) (2:1) = 'O' 
CCLAUS*        MOVE 1   TO IRP03-PAX-SUB
CCLAUS*        MOVE 1   TO IRP03-PROTECT-PRICE-IND (IRP03-PAX-SUB)  
CCLAUS*       PERFORM 2975-GET-PAX-PMR1-AMT THRU 2975-EXIT  
CCLAUS*        MOVE 0   TO IRP03-PROTECT-PRICE-IND (IRP03-PAX-SUB)  
CCLAUS*        MOVE 0   TO IRP03-PAX-SUB
C69559*       MOVE PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2)  
CCLAUS*            IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT  < 2   
CCLAUS*                AND RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT (WS-SUB2) >= 2 
CCLAUS*                COMPUTE WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2) =   
CCLAUS*                PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT * RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT (WS-SUB2)
CCLAUS*            ELSE 
CCLAUS*                MOVE PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2) 
CCLAUS*            END-IF   
CCLAUS*       ADD 1 TO WS-SUB2  
CCLAUS*    ELSE 
CCLAUS*       PERFORM 2180-FETCH-NEXT-PAX  THRU 2180-EXIT   
CCLAUS*       GO TO 2175-OBC-EXIT   
CCLAUS*    END-IF.  
C19412  
C19412 2180-FETCH-NEXT-PAX. 
C34014     ADD 1 TO WS-DK-4.
C34014     IF WS-DK-4 > RSP06-PAX-CNT   
C34014        GO TO 2180-EXIT.  
C34014     IF SELRET-EDOCS AND RSP06-PAX-TICKET-DATE (WS-DK-4) > ZEROES 
EEDOCS         MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EDOCS-PAX-TICKET-IND  
EEDOCS     END-IF.  
C19412  
CCLAUS*    IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-CODE (WS-DK-4)(2:1) = 'O'  
CCLAUS*        MOVE 1   TO IRP03-PAX-SUB
CCLAUS*        MOVE 1   TO IRP03-PROTECT-PRICE-IND (IRP03-PAX-SUB)  
CCLAUS*       PERFORM 2975-GET-PAX-PMR1-AMT THRU 2975-EXIT  
CCLAUS*        MOVE 0   TO IRP03-PROTECT-PRICE-IND (IRP03-PAX-SUB)  
CCLAUS*        MOVE 0   TO IRP03-PAX-SUB
C69559*       MOVE PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2)  
CCLAUS*            IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT  < 2   
CCLAUS*                AND RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT (WS-SUB2) >= 2 
CCLAUS*                COMPUTE WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2) =   
CCLAUS*                PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT * RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT (WS-SUB2)
CCLAUS*            ELSE 
CCLAUS*                MOVE PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-SUB2) 
CCLAUS*            END-IF   
PPC470*       IF RSP06-POBC-EXIST-IND (WS-DK-4) = 'Y'   
PPC470*          PERFORM VARYING WS-POBC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
PPC470*                 UNTIL WS-POBC-SUB > 
PPC470*                         RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT (WS-DK-4)  
PPC470*             IF RSP06-POBC-SRC-CODE  
PPC470*                        (WS-DK-4 WS-POBC-SUB) = 'P'  
PPC470*                MOVE RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT  
PPC470*                        (WS-DK-4 WS-POBC-SUB)
PPC470*                                TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-DK-4)  
PPC470*                MOVE RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT (WS-DK-4)  
PPC470*                                TO WS-POBC-SUB   
PPC470*             END-IF  
PPC470*          END-PERFORM
PPC470*       ELSE  
PPC470*          MOVE ZEROES        TO WS-PAX-OBC-AMT (WS-DK-4) 
PPC470*       END-IF.   
C19412        ADD 1 TO WS-SUB2. 
C19412      GO TO 2180-FETCH-NEXT-PAX.

LOYLTY              PYDR-PAYMENT-TYPE = 'LPP'

LOYLTY                      WS-LYT-POINT-PAY-AMT + PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT 
LOYLTY              IF (PYDR-PAYMENT-DATE > 
LOYLTY                 USEDCC-CC-TRAN-DATE(LPSUB)) OR

LOYLTY                      USEDCC-CC-LAST-NAME (LPSUB) 
LOYLTY                 MOVE PYDR-CARD-HOLDER-FIRST-NAME TO  
LOYLTY                      USEDCC-CC-FIRST-NAME (LPSUB)

LOYLTY                      USEDCC-CC-TRAN-DATE(LPSUB)

LOYLTY              END-IF  
LOYLTY           END-IF 
LOYLTY        END-IF

LOYLTY        IF WS-ELIGBLE-AMT  > 0 AND WS-LYT-POINT-PAY-AMT > 0

LOYLTY                                    WS-LYT-POINT-PAY-AMT

LOYLTY           IF WS-LYT-BAL-DUE < 0  
LOYLTY              MOVE 0 TO WS-LYT-BAL-DUE
LOYLTY           END-IF 
LOYLTY           MOVE WS-LYT-BAL-DUE         TO WS-ELIGBLE-AMT  
LOYLTY        ELSE  
LOYLTY           IF WS-ELIGBLE-AMT < 0  
LOYLTY              MOVE 0 TO WS-ELIGBLE-AMT
LOYLTY           END-IF 
LOYLTY        END-IF
LOYLTY        COMPUTE WS-INELIGBLE-AMT = WS-GROSS-FARE-AMT -
LOYLTY                                      WS-ELIGBLE-AMT  
LOYLTY

PPC470*                IF (RSP06-PAX-PRICE-TYPE (WS-BV-2) = 
PPC470*                                           PMR1-PRICE-TYPE)  AND

PPC470*                                           PMR1-PRICE-CODE)

122022*                     TO PNOKID-FIRST-NAME    (PNOKID-SUB).

122022*                     TO PNOKID-RELATION      (PNOKID-SUB).

135549     MOVE 0           TO WS-PICR-SUB. 
135549 6088-PICR-SELECT-NOK.

135549     ADD 1 TO PNOKID-SUB. 
135549     IF PNOKID-SUB > PNOKID-SEGMENT-MAX   
135549        GO TO 6088-PICR-EXIT.

135549                      TO PNOKID-PAX-SEQ-NUM   (PNOKID-SUB).

135549                      TO PNOKID-FIRST-NAME    (PNOKID-SUB).   
135549     MOVE RSP06-PICR-LAST-NAME     (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)  
135549                      TO PNOKID-LAST-NAME     (PNOKID-SUB).   
135549     MOVE RSP06-PICR-RELATIONSHIP  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)  
135549                      TO PNOKID-RELATION      (PNOKID-SUB).   
135549     IF RSP06-PICR-PHONE-CNT       (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB) > 0  
135549        MOVE RSP06-PICR-PHONE-TYPE (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB 1)
135549                      TO PNOKID-PHONE-TYPE    (PNOKID-SUB)
135549        MOVE RSP06-PICR-PHONE-NUM  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB 1)
135549                      TO PNOKID-PHONE-NUMBER  (PNOKID-SUB)
135549     END-IF.

135549     EXIT.

PPC470     IF  WS-SSCIR-SHIP-CODE     = TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND 
PPC470         WS-SSCIR-SAIL-DATE     = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD AND
PPC470         WS-SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS 
207857         CONTINUE 
207857     ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE        TO SSCIR-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO SSCIR-SAIL-DATE   
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS    TO SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS   
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-SHIP-CODE        TO SSCIR-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
PPC470                                     TO SSCIR-SAIL-DATE   
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS    TO SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS   
207857         FETCH SAIL-STAGGERED-CHECK-IN-REC RECORD 
207857                    ON ERROR GO TO 9810-SCCR-NOT-SET-UP   
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE        TO WS-SSCIR-SHIP-CODE
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO WS-SSCIR-SAIL-DATE
PPC470*        MOVE SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS    TO WS-SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-SHIP-CODE        TO WS-SSCIR-SHIP-CODE
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
PPC470                                     TO WS-SSCIR-SAIL-DATE
PPC470         MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS    TO WS-SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS
207857     END-IF.  

207857*    MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE        TO SSCIR-SHIP-CODE   
207857*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO SSCIR-SAIL-DATE   
207857*    MOVE SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS    TO SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS   
207857*    FETCH SAIL-STAGGERED-CHECK-IN-REC RECORD 
207857*        ON ERROR GO TO 9810-SCCR-NOT-SET-UP. 
207857  
P90198     IF SSCIR-CHECK-IN-IND = 'Y'  
P90198        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHECKIN-ENABLED-IND
P90198     ELSE 
P90198        GO TO 9810-SCCR-NOT-SET-UP
P90198     END-IF.  

207857*    MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE TO WS-SSCIR-SHIP-CODE.  
207857*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE TO WS-SSCIR-SAIL-DATE.  
207857*    MOVE SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS TO WS-SSCIR-DURATION-DAYS.  
P90198  
P90198 9810-VALIDATE-SCIR-TABLE.
207857*    MOVE 'N' TO WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID.   
207857*    PERFORM VARYING WS-SSCIR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
207857*        UNTIL WS-SSCIR-SUB > SSCIR-TABLE-CNT OR  
207857*        WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID  = 'Y' 
207857*        IF SSCIR-TABLE-ID (WS-SSCIR-SUB) =   
207857*           RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-TABLE-ID  
207857*           MOVE WS-SSCIR-SUB  TO SSCIR-SUB   
207857*           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID 
207857*        END-IF   
207857*    END-PERFORM  
207857  
207857     MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID 
207857                                    WS-SSCIR-VALID-DEBARK-ID. 
207857  
207857     IF RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-TABLE-ID > SPACES 
207857        PERFORM VARYING WS-SSCIR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
207857                UNTIL   WS-SSCIR-SUB > SSCIR-TABLE-CNT OR 
207857                        WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID  = 'Y'  
207857           IF SSCIR-TABLE-ID (WS-SSCIR-SUB) = 
207857              RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-TABLE-ID                 AND
207857              SSCIR-EMBARK-DEBARK-IND (WS-SSCIR-SUB) = 'E'
207857              MOVE WS-SSCIR-SUB  TO SSCIR-SUB 
207857              MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-SSCIR-VALID-ID 
207857           END-IF 
207857        END-PERFORM   
207857     END-IF   
207857  
207857     IF RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-DEBARK-TBL-ID > SPACES
207857        PERFORM VARYING WS-SSCIR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
207857                UNTIL   WS-SSCIR-SUB > SSCIR-TABLE-CNT OR 
207857                        WS-SSCIR-VALID-DEBARK-ID  = 'Y'   
207857            IF SSCIR-TABLE-ID (WS-SSCIR-SUB) =
207857               RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-DEBARK-TBL-ID           AND
207857               SSCIR-EMBARK-DEBARK-IND (WS-SSCIR-SUB) = 'D'   
207857               MOVE WS-SSCIR-SUB TO WS-SSCIR-DEB-SUB  
207857               MOVE 'Y'          TO WS-SSCIR-VALID-DEBARK-ID  
207857            END-IF
207857        END-PERFORM   

207857     END-IF   
207857  
P90198      GO TO 9810-VALID-SSCIR-DATA-EXIT.   
P90198 9810-SCCR-NOT-SET-UP.
P90198     MOVE 'N' TO WS-CHECKIN-ENABLED-IND.  
P90198 9810-VALID-SSCIR-DATA-EXIT.  
P90198     EXIT.

242205 9850-FETCH-ITEM-DISC-DTLS.   
242205  
242205     MOVE ZEROES            TO OGSUB. 
242205  
242205     PERFORM VARYING BKMISC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
242205        UNTIL BKMISC-SUB > 5  
242205        MOVE SPACES         TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                   (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)
242205     END-PERFORM. 
242205  
242205     COPY RSP05GRPINT.
242205     IF WS-DK-4 = 1   
242205        IF RSP06-PAX-GIFT-IND (1) = 'Y' OR
242205           RSP06-PAX-GIFT-IND (2) = 'Y' OR
242205           RSP06-PAX-GIFT-IND (3) = 'Y' OR
242205           RSP06-PAX-GIFT-IND (4) = 'Y' OR
242205           RSP06-PAX-GIFT-IND (5) = 'Y'   
242205           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM
242205                            TO RSP05-I-BOOK-NUM   
242205  
242205           CALL 'RSO05GRP' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS 
242205                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO
242205                                 RSP05-INPUT-PARAMS   
242205                                 RSP05-OUTPUT-PARAMS  
242205                                 RSP06-GUEST-CABIN-INFO   
242205  
242205           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND   
242205              DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
242205              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR  
242205           END-IF 
242205  
242205           MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-RSP-CALL-IND
242205        END-IF
242205     END-IF.  
242205  
242205     MOVE 0                 TO BKMISC-SUB.
242205  
242205     IF WS-RSP-CALL-IND = 'Y' 
242205        IF RSP05-CAB-GIFT-CODE-CNT > ZEROES   
242205           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
242205                UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP05-CAB-GIFT-CODE-CNT
242205              IF RSP05-CAB-ALT-GIFT-IND (WS-SUB) = 'Y'
242205                 ADD 1      TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205                 MOVE RSP05-CAB-GIFT-CODE(WS-SUB) 
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205              ELSE
242205                 IF RSP05-CAB-GUEST-SUB(WS-SUB) = WS-DK-4 
242205                    ADD 1   TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205                    MOVE RSP05-CAB-GIFT-CODE(WS-SUB)  
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205                 END-IF   
242205              END-IF  
242205           END-PERFORM
242205        END-IF
242205        IF RSP05-PAX-GIFT-CODE-CNT (WS-DK-4) > ZEROES 
242205           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
242205                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP05-PAX-GIFT-CODE-CNT(WS-DK-4)
242205             ADD 1          TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205             MOVE RSP05-PAX-GIFT-CODE (WS-DK-4 WS-SUB)
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205           END-PERFORM
242205        END-IF
242205     END-IF.  
242205     COPY CCP12BKGINT.
242205     IF WS-RSP-CALL-IND = 'N' 
242205        MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (WS-DK-4)   
242205                            TO CCP12-IN-RATE  
242205        MOVE RSP06-PAX-AGE (WS-DK-4)  
242205                            TO CCP12-IN-AGE   
242205        MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS  
242205                            TO CCP12-IN-DURATION  
242205        MOVE TDP01-EMBARK-PORT-CODE   
242205                            TO CCP12-IN-PORT  
242205  
242205        PERFORM CCP12-FIND-GIFT THRU CCP12-FIND-GIFT-EXIT 
242205  
242205        IF CCP12-OUT-GUEST-TYPE = 'A' 
242205           ADD 1            TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205           MOVE CCP12-OUT-ITEM-CODE   
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205           IF CCP12-OUT-SECOND-ITEM = 'Y' 
242205              ADD 1         TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205              MOVE CCP12-OUT-SECOND-ITEM  
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205           END-IF 
242205        END-IF
242205  
242205        IF CCP12-OUT-GUEST-TYPE = 'F' 
242205           IF WS-DK-4 < 3 
242205              ADD 1         TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205              MOVE CCP12-OUT-ITEM-CODE
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205              IF CCP12-OUT-SECOND-ITEM = 'Y'  
242205                 ADD 1      TO BKMISC-SUB 
242205                 MOVE CCP12-OUT-SECOND-ITEM-CODE  
242205                            TO BKGINF-ITEM-NUM
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205              END-IF  
242205           END-IF 
242205        END-IF
242205     END-IF.  
242205  
242205     MOVE 'N'               TO WS-FOUND-IND.  
242205     PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
242205        UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT  
242205        IF RSP06-OFFER-ID (WS-SUB) = RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (WS-DK-4) 
242205           AND RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM (WS-SUB) = 
242205                                      RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-DK-4) 
242205           MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-FOUND-IND   
242205           MOVE WS-SUB      TO OGSUB  
242205           MOVE RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT 
242205                            TO WS-SUB 
242205        END-IF
242205     END-PERFORM. 
242205  
242205     PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
242205        UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT  
242205        IF RSP06-OFFER-ID (WS-SUB) = RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (WS-DK-4) 
242205           AND RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM (WS-SUB) <= ZEROES 
242205           MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-FOUND-IND   
242205           MOVE WS-SUB      TO OGSUB  
242205           MOVE RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT 
242205                            TO WS-SUB 
242205        END-IF
242205     END-PERFORM. 
242205  
242205     IF WS-FOUND-IND = 'Y'
242205        GO TO 9860-FETCH-DISC-DTLS
242205     END-IF.  
242205  
242205 9860-FETCH-DISC-DTLS.
242205  
242205     PERFORM VARYING BKMISC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
242205        UNTIL BKMISC-SUB > 10 
242205        MOVE SPACES         TO  BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE  
242205                                    (BKGINF-SUB BKMISC-SUB)   
242205     END-PERFORM. 
242205  
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-1(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 1).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-2(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 2).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-3(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 3).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-4(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 4).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-5(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 5).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-6(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 6).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-7(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 7).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-8(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 8).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-9(OGSUB)
242205                           TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 9).
242205     MOVE  RSP06-TAG-CODE-10(OGSUB)   
242205                          TO BKGINF-DISCOUNT-CODE (BKGINF-SUB 10).
